## Document 001: 10月PMI数据解读：10月制造业PMI下滑至荣枯线之下

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月PMI数据解读：10月制造业PMI下滑至荣枯线之下.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年 11月 1日
事件
10 月 31 日，国家统计局服务业调查中心公布，10 月制造业
采购经理指数（PMI）为 49.3，非制造业商务活动指数为 49.7，综
合 PMI产出指数为 49.7。
解读
一、10 月制造业 PMI 下滑至荣枯线之下。
10 月，制造业采购经理指数（PMI）为 49.3%，比 9 月下降
0.8个百分点，年内第 3次下滑至收缩区间。
从分类指数看，构成制造业 PMI 的 5 个分类指数中，供应商
配送时间指数高于临界点；生产指数、新订单指数、原材料库存指
数和从业人员指数低于临界点。
生产指数为 49.8%，比 9月下降 1.9个百分点，表明制造业生
产活动有所放缓。
新订单指数为 49.4%，比 9月下降 1.1个百分点，表明制造业
市场需求景气度下降。
原材料库存指数为 48.4%，比 9月上升 0.2个百分点，表明制
造业主要原材料库存量降幅有所收窄。
从业人员指数为 48.4%，与 9月持平，表明制造业企业用工景
气度总体平稳。

供应商配送时间指数为 50.2%，比 9月上升 0.1个百分点，表
明制造业原材料供应商交货时间加快。
10 月主要原材料购进价格指数为 56.8，比 9 月下滑 4.0 个百
分点，持续高于荣枯线，出厂价格指数为 51.7，比 9月下滑 2.3个
百分点，上游原材料购进价格持续上涨，出厂价格继续上涨，上游
企业效益继续好转。
二、10 月非制造业 PMI 下滑至荣枯线之下。
10 月，非制造业商务活动指数为 49.7%，比 9 月下降 0.5 个
百分点，年内第 4次下滑至荣枯线之下，非制造业景气水平有所下
滑。
宏观研究
10月制造业 PMI下滑至荣枯线之下
——10月 PMI数据解读
市场表现截至 2026.10.31
数据来源：国家统计局,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明
登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

分行业看，建筑业商务活动指数为 48.0%，比 9月下降 2.3个
百分点；服务业商务活动指数为 50.0%，比 9月下降 0.2个百分点。
从服务业行业看，铁路运输、水上运输、邮政、互联网软件及信息
技术服务等行业商务活动指数均位于 55.0%以上较高景气区间；资
本市场服务、保险、房地产等行业商务活动指数均低于临界点。
新订单指数为 44.4%，比 9月下降 2.1个百分点，表明非制造
业市场需求景气水平回落。分行业看，建筑业新订单指数为 40.0%，
比 9月下降 5.7个百分点；服务业新订单指数为 45.2%，比 9月下
降 1.5个百分点。
投入品价格指数为 51.5%，比 9月下降 1.0个百分点，表明非
制造业企业用于经营活动的投入品价格总体水平涨幅收窄。分行业
看，建筑业投入品价格指数为 52.4%，比 9月下降 1.6个百分点；
服务业投入品价格指数为 51.4%，比 9月下降 0.8个百分点。
销售价格指数为 49.5%，比 9月下降 0.8个百分点，表明非制
造业企业销售价格总体水平有所回落。分行业看，建筑业销售价格

指数为 48.9%，比 9月上升 0.4个百分点；服务业销售价格指数为
49.6%，比 9月下降 1.0个百分点。
从业人员指数为 46.0%，比 9月上升 0.1个百分点，表明非制
造业企业用工景气度略有改善。分行业看，建筑业从业人员指数为
42.0%，比 9月下降 2.3个百分点；服务业从业人员指数为 46.7%，
比 9月上升 0.5个百分点。
业务活动预期指数为 54.7%，比 9月下降 0.8个百分点，仍高
于临界点，表明非制造业企业对市场发展保持信心。分行业看，建
筑业业务活动预期指数为 52.3%，比 9月下降 3.1个百分点；服务
业业务活动预期指数为 55.2%，比 9月下降 0.3个百分点。
三、10 月综合 PMI 产出指数下滑至荣枯线之下。
10 月，综合 PMI 产出指数为 49.7%，比 9 月下滑 1.0 个百分
点，下滑至临界点之下，我国企业生产经营活动总体比 9月小幅收
缩。
四、预测 11月制造业景气小幅上升。
10月制造业和非制造业业务活动预期指数分别为 53.4和 54.7，

企业对未来生产经营前景比较乐观，预测 11月制造业景气小幅上
升，非制造业景气继续保持稳定。
投资建议
1：10月制造业和非制造业业务活动预期双双保持乐观。
2：铁路运输、水上运输、邮政、互联网软件及信息技术服务
等行业商务活动指数均位于 55.0%以上较高景气区间。
3：10月制造业出厂价格指数继续上涨，制造业企业效益好转。

### Page 3

风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；
2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

## Document 003: 10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
美东时间 11 月 6 日，美国劳工部统计局（BLS）发布 10 月非农就业报告。新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显
著高于市场预期的 6.5万人；失业率下行至 4.1%，低于市场预期的 4.2%。
评论：
图1：非农数据总览
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
美国 10 月非农大幅超预期。美国 10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，高于预期的 6.5 万人，8-9 月合计上修
8.4万人，显示此前招聘放缓的程度被高估。商务服务、私人教育医疗和运输仓储贡献约九成净新增就业，
咨询、临时用工、医疗及快递招聘构成主要支撑，部分消费服务和地产相关行业仍然承压。我们认为，
美国就业“喜忧参半”，喜的是美国总体就业市场十分强劲，而忧的是就业质量仍存在瑕疵。失业率降至
4.1%，劳动参与率升至 61.9%，就业增长快于劳动力扩张，且裁员压力有限，说明用工需求仍有韧性。不
过，非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率 U6升至 7.9%，表明部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，数据成色有瑕疵。
数据公布后，12 月加息预期升温，但资产表现分化，市场未全面交易持续收紧。站在当前时点，市场对

就业数据的反应存在两难：过弱容易引发衰退交易，过强又会增加激进加息的担忧。本月数据明显偏强，
2026年11月08日

### Page 2

缓解了增长担忧，也使政策收紧重新成为市场关注点。不过，从盘中表现看，其影响主要集中于强化 12
月加息预期（12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%），尚未体现为对明年更大幅度收紧的全面定价。美
债收益率冲高后，长端回吐涨幅，短端仍高于公布前水平；美元走强、黄金下跌，美股开盘后反弹但随
后有所回落。就业韧性缓解了增长担忧，同时增加了利率对资产估值的约束。
这份非农是否属于沃什在 10 月会议上所说的“需要等待的更多信息”？我们认为，确实如此。就业回暖
印证了经济韧性，减轻了进一步收紧的就业顾虑，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。但它是否足以改变政策路
径，成为决定后续加息幅度的关键变量？我们认为，尚不足以。一是非农数据修订较大，单月改善仍需
多项指标相互印证，或连续数月形成趋势；二是在就业保持韧性、价格压力尚未消退的背景下，通胀仍
是政策权衡的重点，就业改善提供了加息空间，却不能单独决定加息幅度；三是对市场而言，民主党掌
控国会后的财政政策变化、AI 投资及回报前景，会更直接地影响中期增长和利率预期，其重要性超过单

月宏观数据。我们维持 12 月加息 25个基点、明年再加息一次的基准判断。
 新增非农：远超预期，招聘改善仍有结构分化
10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显著高于 6.5 万人的市场预期，且 8-9 月合计上修 8.4 万人，显示招聘
修复强于此前判断。我们认为，企业经营活动扩张与用工需求回补是招聘改善的重要支撑，制造业和服
务业 ISM就业指数均处于扩张区间，也提供了旁证。此外，年末旺季招聘为就业提供有力支撑。
从结构看，医疗、商务服务和物流主导本月就业增长，但行业改善仍不均衡。10 月私营部门新增就业
15.9 万人，其中服务业增加 14.4 万人，商品生产增加 1.5万人；政府部门增加 0.6万人。专业商业服务
增加 7.0万人，主要由咨询和临时用工带动；私人教育医疗增加 5.3万人，其中医疗保健增加 4.16 万人，
医院及居家医疗是主要来源；运输仓储增加 2.48 万人，主要由快递招聘支撑，仓储就业则略有减少。上
述三个行业合计贡献约九成净新增就业，而休闲酒店、批发和建筑分别减少 1.3万、0.91万和 0.3万人，

表明招聘修复尚未覆盖全部消费服务及地产相关环节。
图2：新增非农就业趋势 图3：新增非农就业季节性因素
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理

### Page 3

图4：分行业新增非农就业 图5：本月新增就业人数较上月变化
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
 失业率：就业吸纳改善，广义失业率逆势上升
10 月就业吸纳改善，失业率小幅下降。失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，劳动参与率同步升至 61.9%，说明更多
人进入求职市场后，就业增长仍能消化新增供给。从绝对就业人数上看，就业增长快于劳动力增长。10
月劳动力增加 42.1万人，就业增加 46.3万人，失业人数减少 4.1 万人，就业人口比升至 59.3%。我们认
为，失业率回落伴随着参与率和就业人口比同步上升，反映新增劳动力得到更充分吸收。
不过，广义失业率 U6 由 7.6%升至 7.9%，表明岗位数量改善的同时，部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，就业
修复的质量弱于总量表现。失业者分项中，职位流失者及临时工作结束者合计减少 3.4 万人，其中临时
裁员人数减少 7.9 万人、永久职位流失者减少 1.7 万人，临时工作结束者增加 6.2 万人。再进入者增加

0.8万人、新进入者减少 4.0万人，可见新增求职者积压有所缓解。
我们认为，从 10月非农数据来看，美国就业喜忧参半。喜的是新增就业回升、失业率下降，且裁员压力
有限，用工需求仍有韧性；忧的是非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率上升，就业质量改善滞后于数量增长。
往后看，我们更关注国会易手后的预算拨款和政府采购。若两党分歧拖慢项目审批与资金到位，依赖公
共项目的工程建设、科研及专业服务企业将承受订单兑现压力，招聘与就业也将承压。
图6：美国失业率 图7：美国劳动参与率与新增登记失业人数
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理

### Page 4

图8：不同人群失业率 图9：非农平均时薪增速
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
 往后看：近期加息预期强化，中期路径仍取决于通胀
非农数据公布后，市场上调 12 月加息预期。12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%，较前一日提高约 10
个百分点，表明就业回暖增强了市场对联储继续收紧的判断。2027 年仍定价加息一次，市场对本轮加息
的终点仍未形成一致预期。资产价格先交易加息预期升温，随后出现分化，强非农尚未推动远期利率全
面上行。数据发布后 1 小时，2 年期美债收益率上行约 2.9个基点，10 年和 30 年期收益率则在冲高后分
别回落约 0.4 和 2.3 个基点；美元小幅走强，黄金下跌约 34 美元。纳指和标普 500 开盘后一度较开盘上
涨约 0.7%和 0.8%，随后回吐部分涨幅。
我们认为，此次非农对市场的直接影响有限，但确实是沃什等待的重要数据之一。就业回暖进一步印证
经济韧性，使联储能够更多关注通胀，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。不过，油价回落并不意味着通胀压力

已经消退，核心商品和服务价格仍有黏性。对市场而言，国会易手后的财政政策走向、AI 投资能否持续，
是比单月非农更重要的中期主线。这些变化会先影响市场对未来增长和利率的判断，再逐步体现在就业
等宏观数据中。
我们延续先前判断，预计 12 月加息 25 个基点，明年加息一次。我们判断，油价回落将逐步缓解整体通
胀压力，但核心商品成本传导尚未结束，服务价格也仍有黏性，即便能源降温难以成为美联储立即停止
加息的充分理由。当前就业韧性减轻了联储对进一步收紧冲击经济的顾虑，使其能够更多关注价格稳定。
我们暂以 12 月和明年各加息一次为基准，是否进一步收紧，仍取决于价格压力消退的程度。
图10：CME利率期货数据

2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報）予測 
～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～ 
個人消費は小幅減だった一方、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げ

経済調査部 エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁 
  チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司

[要約] 
◼ 11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋
1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する。個人消費は小幅
に減少したが、設備投資と輸出の増加が実質GDPを押し上げたようだ。 
◼ 個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想す
る。設備投資は 4-6 月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。
公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加したと見込んでいる。 
◼ 輸出と輸入はサービスが減少した一方、財が増加したと予想する。代替調達の進展に
よる鉱物性燃料輸入増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が行
われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質 GDP成長率を押し上げた分が相殺したようだ。

図表 1：2026年 7-9月期 GDP予測表

1．2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋1.5％を予想 
11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋1.5％
（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する（10月30日正午までに公表された
基礎統計に基づく）。個人消費は小幅に減少したが、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げたよ
うだ。 
個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想する。設
備投資は4-6月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。 
輸出と輸入はサービスが減少したものの、財が増加したと予想する。中東産原油の代替調達
の進展による鉱物性燃料輸入の増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が
行われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質GDP成長率を押し上げた分で相殺されたようだ。

図表 2：実質 GDP成長率と需要項目別寄与度の推移

民需：個人消費、住宅投資は減少の一方、設備投資は大幅な増加を予想 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％と予想する。4-6月期のインバウンド（個人消費の控除項目）の実
績が上方改定されるであろうことを踏まえると、2 四半期連続のマイナスとなると見込んでい
る。サービスは増加したものの、耐久財と半耐久財、非耐久財がそろって減少したとみている。
耐久財では、自動車税の環境性能割廃止やエアコンの省エネ基準の引き上げの影響もあって 4-
6 月期に増加していた自動車や家電への支出が減少したようだ。半耐久財・非耐久財では衣服
やたばこ、ガソリンなどが増加したものの、食料品などが減少したとみられる。サービスは外
食や鉄道旅客、娯楽などが増加したようだ。 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.8％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7月の建設工事費デフレーター
（住宅総合、大和総研による季節調整値）が前月比＋6.8％と高い伸びを示す中にあっても、7、
8 月平均の新設住宅着工戸数（国土交通省による季節調整値）は、分譲住宅の増加に支えられ
-1.0

### Page 3

3 / 7

4-6月平均比＋1.0％となった（巻末の関連指標②上段右）。しかし、進捗ベースで計上される
GDP統計上は、建設資材等の価格上昇の影響により実質では減少したとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と予想する。建設投資は減少したものの、機械投資と知的財産生
産物投資（ソフトウェア、研究開発など）が増加したようだ。研究開発投資の増加には、4-6
月期に特殊要因で研究開発サービスの受取（設備投資の控除項目）が大幅に増加したことの反
動も含まれる。経済産業省によると、資本財総供給指数は、国産品と輸入品が共に増加し、7-
9 月期は前期比＋4.9％となった（巻末の関連指標②中段左）。一方、建設投資は、国土交通省
によると、民間非居住用建築・土木の出来高（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）の7、8月
平均は 4-6月平均比＋1.2％となったが、7月の建設工事費デフレーター（非住宅総合、大和総
研による季節調整値）は前月比＋7.3％と大きく上昇しており、実質では減少したとみられる。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.0％ptと予想する。仮置き値の原材料在庫は同＋0.1％pt、
仕掛品在庫は同▲0.1％pt となった。その他では製品在庫が同＋ 0.1％pt、流通在庫が同▲
0.1％ptだったとみられる。

公需：公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加と予想 
公共投資は前期比▲1.0％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7、8 月平均の公共工事出来高
（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）は 4-6月平均比▲2.2％と減少傾向にある（巻末の関連
指標②中段右）。25年度補正予算に盛り込まれた公共事業の執行が一服したとみられる。加え
て、工事費の上昇も重しとなっている。 
政府消費は前期比＋0.2％と予想する。退職金支払いの増加や私立高校授業料の実質無償化、
公立小学校の給食費無償化もあって高い伸びとなった 4-6月期に続き、医療費の増加により 7-
9月期の政府消費も増加したとみられる。 
公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.3％ptと予想する。4-6月期と異なり、7-9月期には大規模
な石油の国家備蓄放出は行われず、公的在庫変動は実質GDP成長率を押し上げたとみられる。

外需：輸入増が輸出増を上回り、外需寄与度は▲0.2％ptを予想 
輸出は前期比＋0.5％と予想する。サービスは減少したものの、財は増加したと見込んでい
る。財では半導体等製造装置や化学製品、鉱物性燃料などが増加したようだ。サービスでは、
4-6月期に大幅に増加していた研究開発サービスの反動減に加え、産業財産権等使用料やイン
バウンドなども減少したとみている。 
輸入は前期比＋1.8％と予想する。輸出と同様、サービスは減少したものの、財は増加した
と見込んでいる。財では、代替調達の進展により鉱物性燃料が大きく増加したほか、電算機類
も増加したようだ。サービスではアウトバウンドは増加したものの、研究開発サービスや著作
権等使用料などが減少したとみられる。

### Page 4

1

4 / 7

輸入増が輸出増を上回り、純輸出（外需）の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は前期比▲0.2％
ptになったと予想する。

2．10-12月期の実質 GDPはおおむね横ばいを予想 
2026年 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比でおおむね横ばいになると見込んでいる。個人
消費は小幅に増加、設備投資は増加すると予想する。輸出はAI関連需要を背景とした米国向け
輸出を中心に増加するものの、輸入の伸びが上回り、外需はマイナスになるとみている。 
個人消費は小幅に増加すると予想する。厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」の現金給与総額は
前年同月比で 3％を超える伸びが続いていることに加え、10月以降は各地で最低賃金が引き上
げられることから、名目賃金の高い伸びは続くだろう。一方、9月の消費者物価指数（除く生
鮮食品）は前年同月比＋2.1％と、8月から 0.4％pt伸び幅が拡大した。政策要因による影響を
差し引いたとしても、食料品等を中心に物価上昇率は高まっており、実質賃金上昇率を押し下
げるだろう。エネルギー価格についても、電気・ガス料金補助は12月使用分（1月請求分）か
ら再開されるものの、現行の補助は 9月使用分（10月請求分）で一旦終了するため、10-12月
期の物価は押し上げられる。加えて、ガソリン補助金の基準価格が12月以降引き上げられるこ
とや、燃料費・原料費調整制度によって資源価格高騰の影響がラグを持って電気・ガス料金に
反映されることも物価の押し上げ要因となろう1。 
設備投資は増加すると予想する。日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）によ
ると、9月調査時点での 2026年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、除く土地、含むソフトウェ
ア・研究開発）は前年度比＋9.3％と、前年の 9月調査時点（同＋9.5％）と同程度の伸びが示
された。建設資材価格と労務費の上昇が建設投資を下押しするものの、省力化投資やAI関連投
資といった設備投資の増加基調は続くと見込む。 
住宅投資は建設資材と労務費上昇の影響を受けて減少すると見込んでいる。公共投資は工事
費の高騰を背景に横ばい圏で推移すると予想する。政府消費は高齢化などに伴う医療費・介護
費増により増加すると見込んでいる。 
輸出は増加すると予想する。米国の設備投資は中東情勢をめぐる不透明感や金利上昇の中に
あっても、AI関連需要を背景に増加が続くだろう。米国内での設備投資の増加は米国向け輸出
の追い風となり、財輸出は増加すると見込んでいる。一方、インバウンドは中国政府による日
本への渡航自粛要請が続く中、小幅な増加にとどまるだろう。 
輸入は増加すると予想する。経済産業省によると、9月の原油調達について、バブ・エル・
マンデブ海峡経由で調達予定であった原油の輸送ルートをスエズ運河経由に切り替えたことで

1 12月以降の電気・ガス料金補助とガソリン補助金に関する政府の対応については、首相官邸「中東情勢を踏
まえた『緊急的激変緩和措置』の今後の対応についての会見」（2026年10月 29日）を参照。

### Page 5

1

5 / 7

到着が遅れた分は備蓄原油の既放出分で対応したという2。10月は前年平月比 10割の調達が実
現できる見通しとのことであり、11月以降も同量を確保できるのであれば、財輸入は押し上げ
られるだろう。 
リスク要因は中東情勢の悪化とAI関連需要の下振れである。中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格が
さらに高騰すれば、物価高を通じて個人消費を一段と下押しするだろう。また、足元の米国経
済では設備投資が景気を下支えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などのIT関連投資
がけん引役となっている。10月半ばにかけて、一部AI関連企業の財務上の脆弱性が意識され、
株価が下落する局面があったが、こうした懸念がAI関連企業全体に広がれば、外部資金への依
存度が高い企業を中心に設備投資が鈍化し得る3。その場合、米国向け輸出が下押しされる可能
性があり、注意が必要だ。

2 詳細は、第 5回中東情勢に伴う重要物資の安定的な供給確保のためのタスクフォース（2026年 10月2日）
における経済産業省提出資料を参照。 
3 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。

營收走勢圖

研究員/游周翰 JEFFERY.YU@uni-psg.com

研究部維持全新買進的投資建議，主因為(1)3Q26 毛利率受光電子
營收佔比以及客供料提升，抵消部分費用及業外逆風，使EPS 優於
研究部預期；(2)4Q26 營收隨基板供應穩定、毛利率隨光電子佔比
提高下 ， 帶動營收獲利持續向上 ；(3)展望未來 ，接收端 200G PD 及
發射端 CW Laser/EML 需求增長下，支撐 2027 年營收獲利高成長。
綜上所述，研究部預估 2026/2027 年營收 47.14 億元(原估 49.02
億元)/75.88 億元(原估 78.77 億元)，EPS 為 6.34 元(原估 6.08
元)/12.94 元(原估 11.63 元)。目標由原先 700 元上修至 770 元，
維持買進投資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收受假期影響，毛利率提升受光電子佔比提升： 
3Q26 營收13.42億元(+39%QoQ，+47%YoY)， 略低於研究部預期
13.92億元，主因為9月營收因日本黃金周及中國國慶長假前
影響基板供應。毛利率44.9%(+6.3pts QoQ，+12pts YoY)，優
於研究部預期42.3%，主要受惠於光電子營收佔比從 2Q26的
27%提升至3Q26的43%，且高毛利客供料佔比提升至整體營收
9%。 營業費用達1.83億元(+23%QoQ，+30%YoY)， 主因為補提了
前兩季不足的員工酬勞所致。業外收益3百萬，主要受到CB評
價損失及利息收入下降所致。稅後EPS達1.8元 ， 優於研究部預
期1.69元。

表一、3Q26 研究部預估與實際差異 
單位：百萬元 實際 研究部原估 差異 
營業收入 1,342 1,392 -4% 
營業利益 419 424 -1% 
稅後淨利 423 312 +36% 
EPS(元) 1.80 1.69 +7% 
資料來源： 全新財報、統一研究部預估

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 4Q26光電子佔比有望超越微電子，毛利率將有望續創新高 ： 
展望4Q26，InP基板已獲取中國政府批准出口，且客戶亦有自
行攜帶基板，因此管理層認 爲4Q26營運將有望持續成長。此
外 ， 光通訊產品持續強勁性 ， 有望於4Q26看到單月光電子佔比
大於微電子 ， 將帶動整體毛利率提升 。 惟因客戶攜帶基板佔比
提高 ， 營收獲利認列將有所調整(營收減少 ， 毛利率提升)，因
此研究部預估 4Q26 營 收 從15.85 億 元 調 降 至14.48 億元
(+7.9%QoQ，+50.8%YoY)，EPS從2.18上調至2.32元。

3. 200G PD需求逐季增加，CW Laser/EML推動發射端成長： 
接收端方面，既有美系大客戶200G PD已進入量產，需求逐季
增加，並且目前沒有因為其他光通訊零組件供應限制而出現
下修 。 相較100G PD，200G PD技術門檻及ASP均明顯優於100G，
隨2027年1.6T光模組滲透率提升，預期成為接收端主要成長
來源。發射端方面，日系CW Laser客戶需求強勁，現有量產產
品以100mW為主 ， 並持續開發200–400mW高功率產品 ； 此外 ， 新
增美系客戶已進入產品認證 ， 未來若順利量產 ， 將帶來額外出
貨貢獻。EML方面，兩家客戶已通過性能驗證，目前進行可靠
度測試 ， 管理層預期2026年底前有機會完成認證 ，2027年有望
開始貢獻明顯營收。

管理層指出 ， 發射端產品毛利率普遍優於接收端 ， 且隨日系CW 
Laser既有客戶成長 、 美系新客戶加入及EML逐步量產 ，2027年
發射端營收有機會超越接收端 。 研究部認為 ， 未來光電子業務
成長不僅來自200G PD出貨增加，也將受惠發射端占比提升及
高功率規格升級，推動營收獲利成長。 
4. 客供料占光電子營收約20%，緩解InP短缺並推升毛利率： 
InP基板供應方面 ， 公司目前約有4–5家供應商 ， 其中部分產能
涉及中國大陸出口審批，1H26後雖已取得超過1萬片基板核准
量，並陸續交付，但管理層表示實際核准量仍低於公司需求，
現有批次預期可支應至1Q27。為確保後續供應，公司已向3家
供應商安排逾7億元基板預付款，主要目的為保留未來產能，
惟相關合約未完全鎖定價格，且預付款不代表一定能取得出
口許可，因此2027年實際供貨量仍有不確定性。

另一方面，3Q26客供料約占光電子營收20%，200G PD及EML等
產品皆透過客供料模式。 管理層表示 ， 目前客戶自行取得基板
順利 ， 未來客供料比重可能持續增加 。 由於客供料模式不認列
基板相關銷售收入 ， 但單片可賺取的絕對毛利大致相當 ， 因此
有利提高產品毛利率，亦可部分降低公司對自行申請基板出
口許可的依賴。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 產能及資金提前準備因應2027年光通訊需求高成長： 
全新目前共有67台MOCVD，其中64台可彈性用於GaAs及InP製
程，另3台為GaN專用設備 。3Q26整體稼動率僅五成多 ， 顯示既
有設備尚有產能調配空間 。 因此 ， 為了因應2027年光通訊需求
成長，全新2H26將部分微電子產能提前轉向InP光電子產品，
惟設備轉換後仍須經過約3–6個月客戶認證，預期相關產能將
於1H27逐步貢獻 。 此外 ， 公司規劃新增2台MOCVD， 預計3Q27到
廠 ， 屆時總機台數將由67台增加至69台 。 研究部認為 ， 既有設
備已具備擴充光電子產能的條件，惟實際出貨成長仍取決於
InP基板取得，設備擴產並非目前主要瓶頸。

資金方面，隨公司提前準備InP產能及增加基板採購，資金需
求亦同步提升。為確保2027年原料供應，已向多家InP基板供
應商安排超過7億元預付款，以提前保留供應商產能。設備方
面，累計至3Q26，設備購置Capex約6.07億元，且有約2.01億
元已簽約但未發生的資本支出。為強化後續原料採購及設備
投資所需的資金調度彈性，全新於10/29決議發行第四次及第
五次可轉換公司債(CB)，面額分別為10億元及5億元，合計15
億元。研究部以500元作為轉換價格假設基礎，若兩檔CB全數
轉換，預估分別新增200萬股及100萬股，合計300萬股。以既
有第三、四次CB全數轉換後約1.91億股為基準，預估將稀釋
1.5%。 研究部認為 ， 本次CB籌資的股本稀釋幅度相對有限 ， 且
有助公司提前確保InP基板供應及擴充有效產能，帶動公司營
運持續向上。

表二、研究部預估調整

資料來源： 統一研究部預估

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言

告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

• Hon Hai's partnership with NVIDIA is extending across GPU, CPUs, LPU, 
DPUs, networking and integrated compute systems. Management 
highlighted more than ten dedicated manufacturing facilities supporting 
Vera Rubin production, underscoring the scale of its preparation. A notable 
engineering breakthrough is the redesign of compute trays to eliminate 
conventional cables, liquid-cooling hoses and fans, simplifying assembly, 
improving reliability and enabling greater automation. Hon Hai also 
showcased its work on robotic assembly, leveraging Omniverse digital 
twins, computer vision and physical AI models to automate complex 
operations previously requiring manual precision. These include busbar 
installation and high-density connector insertion. We believe deeper 
design-for-manufacturing and design-for-automation capabilities should 
help Hon Hai accelerate production ramps, improve manufacturing yields 
and as AI systems become increasingly complex. We also believe this will be 
the core competence for Hon Hai to secure next-generation rack-scale 
systems, where manufacturing complexity and system-level validation 
increasingly differentiate suppliers.
• NVIDIA DSX POD, a factory-integrated, pre-validated modular AI 
infrastructure solution, provided by Hon Hai. Each POD contains seven 
Vera Rubin NVL72 and three Spectrum-6 SPX racks, and incorporating 
more than 10 million components. By completing rack integration, cabling, 
liquid-cooling connections and functional testing before shipment, the 
solution aims to reduce onsite deployment from approximately six months 
to as little as 14 days. NVIDIA expects its partner ecosystem to deliver 
these solutions beginning in early 2027. Hon Hai is also collaborating with

TECO on modular data centers that integrate IT systems, power distribution and cooling within 40-foot containers,
with more than 80% of modular assembly completed at the factory. We believe this model could meaningfully
expand Hon Hai’s revenue opportunity beyond rack assembly into fully integrated infrastructure, while accelerating
time to first revenue-generating AI tokens.
• Hon Hai highlighted its expanding role in next-generation AI networking, including NVIDIA’s Spectrum-6 SN6800

CPO switch, delivering 409.6Tbps aggregate bandwidth through 512 ports at 800Gbps. Hon Hai showcased
capabilities spanning CPO optical components, ELSFP modules, high-speed connectivity and complete switch-tray
integration, positioning itself to capture additional networking content as optical interconnect adoption accelerates.
Next, we expect the scale-up CPO with NVL576 to be announced with VRU next year; Hon Hai will remain the sole
supplier, and in our view, its first-mover advantage will be supported for further revenue and earnings upside.

• Other than VR200 system, Hon Hai also demonstrated Nvidia's LPUs, integrated into its Vera Rubin ecosystem
through LPX inference racks containing 256 LPUs. Unlike GPUs optimized for parallel computation, LPUs
accelerate latency-sensitive token generation, particularly feed-forward and mixture-of-experts execution,
complementing Rubin GPUs handling prefill and attention workloads. We believe the heterogeneous GPU-LPU
computing could expand Hon Hai's addressable market in AI infrastructure integration.

• Beyond AI Data Centers: Hon Hai highlighted progress in EV, autonomous mobility, robotics and smart healthcare.  In
EVs, it introduced the affordable Model A multi-purpose vehicle, targeting Japan and Southeast Asia. Its CDMS
strategy focuses on shortening development cycles and improving cost competitiveness. In robotics, Hon Hai
demonstrated physical AI, humanoid robots and digital-twin-enabled manufacturing automation, supported by its
collaboration with NVIDIA and ABB Robotics.  The company also showcased emerging capabilities in SiC

semiconductors, low-Earth-orbit satellites and FoxBrain AI models. We believe these initiatives reinforce Hon Hai's
longer-term transformation into a diversified technology manufacturing platform.

Hon Hai Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is at the higher end of the historical trading range.
We believe this multiple is justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new
endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus

we believe a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand;
2) intensified peer competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress; and 4) higher-than-expected
impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and

Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Hon Hai Precision Industry

HHTD 2026: precision manufacturing, 
vertical integration, AI DC, and new EV

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 320.00 TWD | Price: 245.50 TWD

7th HHTD: precision manufacturing, vertical integration 
Hon Hai hosted its seventh Tech Day, titled "Accelerating Intelligence" on 30 Oct. Key 
highlights included its strengths in precision manufacturing, vertical integration, robotics 
and AI datacenters, as well as the launch of the Model A+ and Model K/K+ EVs. We 
expect Hon Hai, with its strong manufacturing capability and vertical integration 
advantages, to sustain its leading position as an AI server ODM. Maintain Buy. 
Doubled AI rack production to 2k racks per week currently  
Hon Hai's Chairman indicated that the company has shortened the assembly time for a 
compute tray from 2 hours to 20 minutes. This was supported by its precision 
manufacturing capability (1µm precision), automation and a cableless design co-
developed with its customer. Amid continued strong AI demand, Hon Hai has doubled its 
AI server rack production from 1,000 racks per week a year ago to 2,000 racks per week. 
AI server solution offerings across L1-L12 and CPO 
The company can now provide up to 50% of server rack components (excluding 
GPUs/CPUs), with solution offerings spanning L1 to L12. It demonstrated its high-speed 
cables/connectors, power busbars/whips, liquid cooling solutions (manifolds/cold 
plates/UQDs), compute/switch trays including the NVIDIA Spectrum-X CPO switch tray, 
server racks, as well as SuperPOD and AI datacenter solutions. 
Robotics for factory automation and new EV 
Hon Hai showcased live demonstrations of its robotics, highlighting capabilities such as 
AI-planned assembly paths and real-time sensing and tracking. For EVs, the company 
launched Model A+ and Model K/K+. Model A+ features L4 readiness, four-wheel drive 
and a spacious cabin. Model K converts from a truck into a van with a canopy box, while 
Model K+ features a more stylish design to serve the Japanese and European markets.

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Hon Hai is the world's largest EMS provider, with four key 
business segments - Smart Consumer Electronics, Cloud & 
Networking, Computing, and Components & Others. Hon 
Hai supplies components, final assembly, and global 
logistics for global tech giants, including Apple, Microsoft, 
Google, HP, Dell, Cisco, and Sony. Hon Hai has 
R&D/production sites globally, including Taiwan, China, 
India, Japan, Vietnam, Malaysia, Czech Republic, Mexico, and 
the US. The company was established in Taiwan in 1974.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Hon Hai as its gradual moves into 
high margin/value-added server/components/semi 
business, stronger position in AI server with rising sales, 
solid balance sheet, and attractive valuation among Greater 
China AI server players.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Hon Hai Precision Industry (HNHAF) 
We set our PO at NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS), at around 3SD above historical P/E 
average since 2007. We think the valuation is justified as HH's leading position in 
EMS/ODM business, improving iPhone momentum with replacement cycle, rising sales 
from AI business, more diversified global footprint than peers to mitigate 
tariff/geopolitical risk, and improving balance sheet with higher cash dividend payout.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker momentum from Apple business (iPhone, iPad 
and Apple Watch), (2) increasing competition in EMS business from peers, (3) slower 
sales/earnings delivery in auto/EV business, and (4) weaker market demand or lower 
shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better-than-expected yield rate and demand for iPhone, 
iPad and Apple Watch business, (2) further automation adoption to reduce labor costs 
and improve efficiency, (3) faster sales/earnings delivery from auto/EV business, and (4) 
stronger market demand or higher shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,430 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 21.5x for 2027E, which is at par with its historical average.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China
macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;

and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 171: 261105_daiwa_SDI

Source PDF: all report pdf/261105_daiwa_SDI.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: SDI reported its 3Q26 results after market hours on 5 November. Its 3Q26 EPS 
was TWD1.94, beating our EPS estimate (TWD1.79) but missing the Bloomberg 
consensus (TWD2.07).

We have a Buy (1) rating on SDI with a 12-month TP of TWD265, based on a PER of 35x 
on our 1-year-forward EPS. For more information on the company, please refer to our 
latest flash, 2Q26 review: net profit beat expectations, on 7 August 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results beat our estimate but missed the Bloomberg consensus. SDI’s 3Q26 
revenue of TWD4,020m (+12.9% QoQ, +58.1% YoY) beat our and the consensus 
estimates by 12.4% and 2.4%, mainly due to the better-than-expected auto segment 
and rush orders for the consumer segment. Its gross margin came in at 20.5% in 3Q26 
(vs. 20.7% in 2Q26 and 12.4% in 3Q25), missing both our (20.7%) and the consensus 
(21.5%) estimates, which we attribute to a lower-than-expected yield rate and OT 
expense despite positive factors such as price hikes, a higher copper price and FX. Its 
operating profit of TWD517m (+24.0% QoQ, +399.0% YoY) beat our estimate by 13.4%, 
but was in line with the consensus estimate. Its net profit came in at TWD383m, which 
also beat our estimate (TWD339m) but missed the Bloomberg consensus (TWD400m). 
Its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.94. For 3Q26 revenue, electronics accounted for 91% 
(+14.2% QoQ, +67.3% YoY) and stationery accounted for 9% (+1.6% QoQ, +1.6% YoY). 
Within electronics, the product mix was: auto 47% (+14.2% QoQ, +63.8% YoY), 
industrial 15% (+14.2% QoQ, +19.5% YoY), consumer 28% (+6.6% QoQ, +67.3% YoY), 
AI lead frame 7% (+33.2% QoQ) and others 3% (+71.3% QoQ, +67.3% YoY).

• Bright lead frame outlook with rising AI lead frame. SDI expects 4Q26 revenue to be 
flat to slightly up QoQ and 20%+ revenue YoY growth for its 2027 lead frame business, 
due to an intact order outlook from auto, industrial and AI. It also expects its AI lead 
frame revenue is to reach 10%+ of 2027 revenue.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating on SDI with a 12-month TP of TWD265, based on a PER of 35x 
on our 1-year-forward EPS. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 25.2x/19.1x, based 
on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-3-year one-year forward PER trading range of 12-72x

## Document 230: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다.

## Document 315: CoreWeave致信得州当选州长，寻求“数据中心磋商”

Source PDF: all report pdf/CoreWeave致信得州当选州长，寻求“数据中心磋商”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

在这封公开信中， Michael Intrator 表⽰， CoreWeave“ 强烈⽀持 ”Hinojosa 关于数据中⼼增
⻓必须惠及得克萨斯州⺠众的原则⽴场。 “ 我们欢迎与您的过渡团队合作，共同负责任地推
进已获批项⽬，并就未来开发确⽴清晰、持久的标准 ” ，他表⽰。
摘要：
AI 基础设施公司 CoreWeave 正⾯回应得克萨斯州政策转向，主动寻求与新任州⻓展开对话。
据彭博社 11 ⽉ 8 ⽇报道， CoreWeave 联合创始⼈兼⾸席执⾏官 Michael Intrator 致信得州当选州⻓
Gina Hinojosa ，表⽰欢迎与其过渡团队就 “ 已获批项⽬的负责任推进 ” 及 “ 未来开发的清晰、持久
标准 ” 展开合作。 Hinojosa 是得克萨斯州逾 30 年来⾸位⺠主党州⻓，其核⼼竞选承诺之⼀正是对
新数据中⼼审批实施暂停令，并已公开表态在相关保护性⽴法到位之前不会解除冻结。
这封信发出的背景，是美国中期选举后政治格局的深刻变化。⺠主党在多个州赢得州⻓选举，
并普遍以更严格的数据中⼼监管为竞选纲领。据⾼盛此前预计，⺠主党各州胜选将导致约 45%

的美国计划数据中⼼扩张容量⾯临短期暂停令，其中得克萨斯州单独占约 25% 。约 13GW 的得州
数据中⼼容量直接暴露于政策⻛险。
CoreWeave 表态⽀持监管原则
据报道，在这封公开信中， Michael Intrator 表⽰， CoreWeave“ 强烈⽀持 ”Hinojosa 关于数据中⼼
增⻓必须惠及得克萨斯州⺠众的原则⽴场。
“ 我们欢迎与您的过渡团队合作，共同负责任地推进已获批项⽬，并就未来开发确⽴清晰、持久
的标准 ” ，他表⽰。
Hinojosa 在选后接受 CNN 采访时阐述了其执政理念：
CoreWeave 在信中对上述⽴场作出正⾯回应， Michael Intrator 表⽰：
据华尔街⻅闻⽂章， Hinojosa 于当地时间 11 ⽉ 6 ⽇接受 CNN 采访时就明确表态： “ 我们将制定法
律来保护得州居⺠，在这些保护措施到位之前，我们需要暂停⽬前在数据中⼼⽅⾯的相关⼯作。 ”
她同时补充： “ 我并不是讨厌数据中⼼。我讨厌它们利⽤得克萨斯州的⽅式。当我担任州⻓时，

这种情况将停⽌。 ”
在被问及如何与科技巨头打交道时， Hinojosa 表⽰可以与⻢斯克及其公司建⽴合作关系， " 但必
须按我们的条件来 " 。这⼀表态划定了她的谈判底线：得州不拒绝科技投资，但不接受此前开发
商主导、政府配合的模式。
“ 得克萨斯⼀直是⼀个亲商业的州，但随着我出任州⻓，改变在于这必须是⼀种让得州⼈受益的
合作关系。 ”
“ 您已明确表⽰，数据中⼼的增⻓必须对得克萨斯州有利，我们强烈⽀持这些原则。 ”

写评论
NBC 出⼝⺠调数据显⽰， 75% 的得克萨斯选⺠反对在其社区新建数据中⼼。 Hinojosa 在竞选期
间曾指控数据中⼼开发商 “ 推⾼了我们的电费，耗尽了我们的⽔资源 ” ，并以约 52.8% 对 45.7% 的
得票率击败共和党现任州⻓ Abbott 。
暂停令与⽴法进程挂钩，解冻最早要等到明年 5 ⽉
华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道， Hinojosa 的表态实际上将数据中⼼审批的解冻时间表与⽴法进程直接挂
钩。⾼盛分析师 Jonathan Leitol 此前已确认，她 " 在更全⾯的监管法规通过之前不会解除冻结 " 。
得克萨斯州第 90 届⽴法会议将于 2027 年 1 ⽉ 12 ⽇召开，常规会期于 2027 年 5 ⽉ 31 ⽇结束。这意
味着，数据中⼼审批冻结最早要到明年 5 ⽉底才可能松动。

GLJ Research 估计，约13GW 的得州数据中⼼容量直接暴露于政策⻛险，⾜以为约 1000 万⼾家
庭供电。富国银⾏测算，得克萨斯数据中⼼每减少 10% ，将对全美 AI 资本开⽀造成约 1.6% 的冲
击。
按彭博新能源财经（ BNEF ）估计的 AI 容量每 GW 每⽉约 17.6 亿美元收⼊粗算，若 13GW 延迟 12
⾄ 18 个⽉，被推迟的 AI 营收规模约在 2750 亿⾄ 4100 亿美元之间。
对于 AI 基础设施公司⽽⾔，得州政策⾛向的不确定性直接影响其扩张节奏与资本规划。
CoreWeave 此次主动致信，正是在这⼀背景下寻求政策层⾯的确定性。
得州成 “ 样板 ” ，监管收紧浪潮或向多州蔓延
根据中期选举结果，⺠主党⽬前在全美掌控 29 个州⻓职位，其中多位州⻓正呼吁在各⾃辖区对
数据中⼼建设实施暂停令，以回应选⺠对 AI 基础设施快速扩张的⼴泛不满。
华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道，沃尔夫研究分析师已将得克萨斯定性为 “ 反 AI 建设运动的样板州 ” 。佐治
亚、俄亥俄、密歇根等州的⺠主党新任州⻓，均以更严格的数据中⼼监管为竞选纲领，杰富瑞

预计 “ 反数据中⼼⾏动将在 2027 年持续蔓延⾄多个州⽴法机构 ” 。
Hinojosa 将于 2027 年 1 ⽉正式就职。在此之前， CoreWeave 等 AI 基础设施公司能否通过对话争
取到更明确的政策框架，将是市场持续关注的焦点。

as well as 
 monetary tightening. Nevertheless, despite these 
new challenges, real GDP growth is expected to exceed 7%.  
◼ 
 India is currently in a stronger position than it was in 2022 to cope with the new energy shock. The fiscal leeway to support  
the economy has increased with the decline in the fiscal deficit, and inflation is more contained.  
◼ 
 Although 
 rising  
energy prices have put pressure on external accounts, FX reserves 
 remain  
sufficient to cover the country’s 
short
 -
 term external financing needs. 
 Yet, 
downward pressures on the rupee have been 
 significant 
 and 
could persist in the 
short term
 . 
In 
H1 
2026, India’s external accounts withstood the rise in energy prices fairly well
 , notably  
thanks to the sharp increase 
in workers’ remittances. 
 T
he 
widening of the trade deficit was primarily due to the substantial increase in machinery and 
equipment imports 
 while t
 he oil trade deficit increased 
 only 
moderately, as the rise in energy prices was offset by a decline 
in import volumes. 
 However, 
in H2 2026, the oil trade deficit is expected to widen significantly.  
Additionally, large portfolio outflows have exacerbated downward pressure on the rupee, necessitating interventions by 
the central bank in foreign exchange markets and the adoption of regulatory measures to support the currency. Although 
these measures hav
 e led to a rebound in the rupee (at least temporarily), inflationary pressures, volatile portfolio 
investments, and low net foreign direct investments are 
 expected to continue 
 to put pressure on the currency.  
◼ 
 D
ownward pressures on the rupee 
 and 
 rising bond yields will reduce the government's 
 already limited 
 fiscal capacity to 
increase public investment and 
 support economic development. However, this will not undermine its ability to refinance 
its debt, given its safe structure.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector is sufficiently robust to cope with the expected rise in credit risks.

STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH DESPITE 
STRUCTURAL CONSTRAINTS 
◼ 
 In October 2026, the IMF revised upward 
 India’s  
medium
 -
 term growth 
 forecast 
 from 6.5% to 6.8%  
per year
 . Nonetheless, 
to achieve such strong growth performance, 
 India will have to adapt to the challenges posed by artificial intelligence  
(AI)
, 
while its infrastructure and education system are not prepared for it.  
◼ 
 Moreover, India’s development remains highly uneven. 
 The middle class is still modest
 . This is explained by a large 
informal economy, heavy concentration of employment in low value
 -
 added sectors such as agriculture, low participation 
rates among women and youth, and limited industrial development. All this, in turn, reflects India’s weak i
 ntegration into 
global value chains.  
◼ 
 India faces two key structural challenges: i
 ) adapting the services sector to AI and ii) accelerating industrial development. 
Even with progress in sectors such as smartphones and pharmaceuticals, India’s industrial base remains too small to 
absorb its vast labour force. 
 Labo
u
r market 
 rigidities  
and the mismatch between training and business needs are holding 
back both the creation of quality jobs and industrial development.  
◼ 
 Low net foreign direct investment is a 
 major  
 obstacle. While 
 India attracts 
 new 
 FDI, retention is weak
 : 
 despite 
improvements in logistics, governance issues and regulatory barriers have 
 led 
 disinvestments to triple over the past five 
years.  
◼ 
 India 
 may fail in completing its 
 transition to a higher
 -
 value, more industrialised and inclusive economy if reforms do not 
accelerate 
 significantly
 . Structural change
 s 
could lag behind pressures from its rapidly growing population and 
 fast 
global 
technological advances
 .

INDIA
’S COMPLEX POSITION 
IN INTERNATIONAL TRADE 
Low 
attractiveness  
due to unfavourable business environment and limited integration in global value chains  
◼ 
 Despite very strong growth, India's attractiveness is low relative to the size of its economy. The stock of foreign direct 
investment stood at only 15% of GDP in Q2 2026 compared to 52% of GDP in Vietnam and around 67% of GDP in Thailand 
and Malaysia.  
◼ 
 This lack of appeal can be attributed to 
 India’s still unfavo
 u
rable 
 business environment and 
 its 
 limited integration into 
global value chains. India’s global market share remain
 s 
 low (1.9% of total 
 world 
 exports  
 of goods
 )
, 
 especially in 
comparison with that of China (16.1% of total exports).  
◼ 
 India has not benefited from the AI boom
 , 
since its exports of AI
 -
 related goods remain modest (0.5% of its total exports)
 . 
However, 
 it has benefited from the “China +1” strategy adopted by 
 international 
 companies seeking to diversify their 
production sources. Smartphone exports by India have increased 6.3
 -
 fold over the past five years and accounted for almost 
6% of global exports in 2025 compared to less than 1% five year
 s 
earlier.  
To 
increase its a
 ttractiveness and develop its industrial sector, India has 
 multiplied F
 ree 
T
rade 
 A
greements  
◼ 
 Over the past 18 months, 
 India  
has signed a growing number of free trade agreements 
 – 
with the European Free Trade 
Association in October 2025, the United Kingdom in July 2025, Oman in December 2025, and the European Union in January 
2026
. This 
 should help to develop the industrial base and demonstrates the Modi government's commitment to 
accelerating India's integration into global trade.  
The sectors most likely to benefit from the recent trade agreement with the EU are electronics and semiconductors, 
pharmaceuticals and chemical
 s
, textile and renewable energy technologies. But expanding trade agreements without deep 
domestic improvements in the business environment, governance, education, and infrastructure will be ineffective. India 
will continue to struggle with investment reten
 tion. 
◼ 
 The US
 -
 India bilateral trade agreement has not yet been concluded. The two sides reached a framework for an interim 
Agreement in February 2026, under which the US would apply an 18% reciprocal tariff on Indian goods, with further 
reductions subject to the 
 successful conclusion of the agreement. As of early October, negotiations 
 are 
stalled. Key sticking 
points include market access, particularly 
 in 
India’s sensitive agricultural sector.

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE

Non transfer risk   
India’s external accounts are solid with a low external debt, moderate current account deficit and large FX 
reserves. 
 India’s net debtor position reached only 5.9% of GDP in Q2 2026. In the short term, external accounts 
are expected to deteriorate 
 given 
the rise in energy prices while downward pressures on the rupee should remain 
high.  
◼ 
 External accounts have remained solid 
 despite recent adverse shocks
 . 
 In 2025, external accounts remained solid 
despite the significant increase in US tariffs. In 
 H1 
 2026, they held up well against rising energy prices. Although the 
current account deficit is expected to widen for the current fiscal year as a whole, it should remain contained, and foreign 
exchange reserves should be more than sufficient to cover the co
 untry’s short
 -
 term financing needs. The 
 major  
causes 
for concern are the 
 structurally 
 low level of foreign direct investment (although it is rising) and the increased reliance on 
portfolio investment, which exposes the currency to greater volatility.  
◼ 
 Structural vulnerability to energy price shock
 . 
India is structurally vulnerable to an energy price shock as its 
 oil and gas  
trade deficit is relatively high (2.8% of GDP in 2025). However, in H1 2026, the current account withstood fairly well the 
rise in energy prices especially thanks to the sharp increase in workers’ remittances  
(+0.7 pp to 3.7% of GDP)
 . The trade 
deficit widened significantly but principally because of the strong increase in machinery and equipment imports rather

 energy 
 trade deficit increased moderately as the rise in energy prices was offset by the

However, the trade deficit is expected to widen further during the rest of the year with the significant rise in oil prices, 
and 
 the current account deficit 
 is projected

 still modest and slightly lower than during the previous energy shock

 External debt is low (20.8% of GDP and 87.1% of total exports in Q2 2026). 
 Banks accounted for the largest share of 
external debt (30.9% of total),

 ’s

reached 22.3%.   
◼ 
 Currency risk
 : 
 India is vulnerable to capital outflows and is very exposed to change in US monetary policy. 
 Its 
performance will continue to depend on energy prices and the spread between India’s and the US government bond yields.

Sovereign risk  
Public finances are structurally fragile 
 and fiscal policy room is narrow, 
 as India has one of the lowest tax bases 
in Asia and one of the highest debt interest burdens (second only to Pakistan). Nonetheless, the debt refinancing 
risk is limited given 
 its 
sound 
 profile 
 (
debt 
is 
denominated in domestic currency and held by domestic investors 
with a long maturity).

◼ 
 In FY2026 (ended in March 2026), public finances continued to improve. 
 The central government’s fiscal deficit 
narrowed to 4.4% of GDP from 5% of GDP the previous year. 
 However, t
 his reduction stemmed primarily from a significant 
rise in dividends from the central bank. 
 G
overnment revenues remained low at  
9.6% of GDP, with tax revenues stagnant 
at 
7.6% of GDP. Besides, debt
 -
 servicing costs climbed to a peak of  
3.6% of GDP, equivalent to  
37.6% of total government 
revenue
s
. 
The fiscal position is stronger than during the 2022 energy crisis, with the general government deficit estimated 
at 
7.8% of GDP  
in FY2026, compared to  
9.5% 
in FY2021/2022.  
◼ 
 For FY2027, the initial budget targets a slight reduction in the central 
 government 
 deficit, to 4.3% of GDP. This goal may 
however 
 prove challenging due to rising subsidies amid the energy shock.  
To cushion households from the shock, the government initially 
 kept 
petrol prices 
 unchanged
 . Refining firms absorbed the 
financial burden, partially offset by lower excise duties on petroleum products. Only after regional elections in mid
 -
 May 
did the government allow a modest increase in pump prices to reflect higher international 
 prices
 . 
The government has 
also increased subsidies  
to offset the impact of rising fertiliser prices on farmers.  
Revenue losses  
—
driven by  
lower excise 
duties, reduced dividends from state
 -
 owned enterprises, and the economic slowdown
 — 
could reach  
0.5% of GDP
 , while 
the cost of 
 fertiliser subsidies 
 could 
 rise from  
0.4% to 0.8% of GDP.  
To reduce the risk of fiscal slippage, the government 
has: i) raised taxes on fuel exports, ii) capped household diesel purchases; and iii) planned cuts to certain expenditures.  
◼ 
 While the primary deficit reduction  
 target may prove difficult to achieve, 
 the surge in government bond yields  
 is 
another source of severe concern
 . 
Over the first ten months of 2026
 , 
10
-

 and 
further increase  
is 
expected due to 
 the central bank’s 
 monetary tightening. A  
100
-
 bp 
rise in interest rates  
could push debt  
servicing costs above  
40% of 
fiscal 
 revenue by 2029
 . This is expected to 
 increase the debt
 -
 to
-
 GDP ratio 
 gradually
 .

◼ 
 Despite the high public debt level (84.6% of GDP in FY2026), refinancing risks remain moderate. 
 The debt structure is 
relatively safe, and the government benefits from access to 
 large 
 domestic savings. In Q2 2026, 87.8% of central 
government debt was domestic
 , and 
 70.5% was market debt
 . 
It is 
 almost entirely held by domestic financial institutions 
and denominated in local currency. 
 R
efinancing risks are limited as most debt is long term (the average maturity of central

 -

 Treasury 
 securities in Q2 2026. 
 Finally,

 in foreign currency 
 accounted 
for only 3.0% of total general government debt in FY2025/2026 (
 i.e. 
2.6% of GDP).

Systemic credit risk  
The banking sector has strengthened over the past five years
 , 
as illustrated by the improvement in 
 asset  
quality 
and capital buffers. The main sources of credit risks are rising household debt, the rise in energy prices, the 
unfavourable monsoon and monetary tightening.  
◼ 
 The quality of assets has improved significantly. 
 In Q1 2026, the banking sector’s non
 -
 performing loan (NPL) ratio fell 
to a multi
 -
 decade low of 1.8%. For public banks  
(
54.4% of total loans
 )
, the NPL ratio declined to 2.1%.  
◼ 
 Capital buffers are strong. 
 While 
loan loss provisions remained modest at 75.6% of NPLs, capital buffers stayed strong. 
The Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR) rose to 17.7% in Q1 2026, up from 17.4% a year earlier. From April 2027, Indian banks 
will adopt a new Expected Credit Loss (ECL) framewo
 rk, requiring earlier provisioning for potential loan losses. In the 
short term, higher provisioning requirements could push up lending rates and tighten credit conditions 
 while 
 triggering 
capital raising or targeted government recapitalisation. 
 I
n the medium term, the reform could strengthen banking  
sector 
resilience through earlier recognition of credit risks and more robust loss buffers.  
◼ 
 Banks’ 
 liquidity 
 is 
 comfortable
 . 
It deteriorated slightly in H1 2026 because credit growth outpaced deposit growth
 ; t
he 
liquidity coverage ratio decli
 ned to 
a still solid 
 124% 
and 
the loan
 -
 to
-
 deposit ratio increased to 83% in Q2 2026 from 79.1% 
a year earlier. RBI interventions to support the rupee 
 also 
 drained liquidity
 . Moreover
 , the deposit mix shifted from low
 -
cost current and savings accounts (CASA) to higher
 -
 cost term deposits, pressuring net interest margins. However, since 
June 2026, liquidity has improved significantly due to RBI measures to attract foreign
 -
 currency non
 -
 re
sident (FCNR) 
deposits.  
◼ 
 Profitability remains satisfactory. 
 It 
 has declined since the start of the year, primarily due to narrowing net interest 
margins, but remains satisfactory. In Q2 2026, 
 the 
 return on assets (ROA) and return on equity (ROE) stood at 1.6% and 
13.0%, respectively (IMF data).  
◼ 
 P
rivate
 -
 sector  
 debt 
 is moderate
 , 
 but its increase 
 needs to be monitored.  
 It stood 
 at 105.1% of GDP in Q1 2026 (BIS  
data
), compared to 100.1% five years earlier
 , which is 
 lower than in other emerging Asian economies.  
Non
-
 financial corporat
 e 
debt was 56% of GDP in Q1 2026. Listed firms’ financial positions continued to strengthen amid 
surging profits, improving their ability to service debt. In Q2 2026, the 
 average 
 interest coverage ratio (ICR) rose to 7.4, up 
from 6.1 a year earlier. However, the share of companies with an ICR below 1 (insufficient earnings to cover interest costs) 
increased to 18.5%. Stress remain
 s 
 concentrated in micro
 -
 enterprises, agriculture, and energy
 -
 intensive sectors (
 e.g.
, 
power, steel, cement, aviation), which are 
 the 
most exposed to oil
 -
 price shocks.  
Household leverage requires monitoring. Although still moderate, household debt increased to 49.1% of GDP in Q1 2026, 
up from 42.7% five years earlier, driven largely by non
 -
 housing retail loans (58.4% of total borrowing).  
◼ 
 Monetary 
 policy 
 tightening. 
 Due to rising  
inflation (+5.5% year
 -
 on
-
 year in September) and downward pressure on the 
rupee, the RBI raised its policy rate by 25 basis points to 5.5% in October 2026 under a "calibrated tightening" stance.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector should remain resilient  
 despite rising risks in the short term. 
 Due to rising energy prices, a poor 
monsoon, and monetary tightening, asset quality is expected to deteriorate, particularly for low
 -
 income borrowers, micro, 
and small enterprises. 
 E
arly warning signs are emerging
 , with some 
 borrowers starting to miss payments. The stress is 
concentrated in unsecured retail loans  
(
which account for over half of fresh retail slippages
 )
. 
This could lead to new 
 NPLs 
over the coming quarters, especially as higher rates and inflation squeeze repayment capacity. Additionally, in the near 
term, capital inflows may remain weak, 
 and 
 liquidity and funding pressures could intensify.

## Document 320: CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận -  Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22).pdf

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

## Document 334: Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

8
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
GROWTH ON TRACK DESPITE THE TURBULENCE
Growth was stronger than expected in Q2 and is set to strengthen further in the coming quarters. According to our fore-
casts, it will reach 1.5% in 2027 after 1.1% in 2026. This growth is expected to be driven by investment and its knock-on
effect on exports. On an annual average basis, inflation is expected to stand at 3% in 2026 and 2.8% in 2027, peaking at

the end of 2026. The ECB is expected to continue its monetary tightening. At this stage, a single further rate rise, aimed at
keeping inflation expectations anchored, is expected in December. The impact of rising interest rates (key policy rates and
market rates) on public finances and private sector borrowing conditions will be worth monitoring.
GROWTH: BEYOND THE SHOCKS, A RESILIENT MOMENTUM
Growth in the Eurozone remained robust in the first half of 2026, at

0.25% q/q in Q1 and 0.33% q/q in Q2, excluding Ireland (where economic
activity is volatile). It was driven by Germany, where signs of a recovery
are becoming clearer (see Germany). Our baseline scenario forecasts
growth of 1.1% in 2026, followed by 1.5% in 2027 on an annual average
basis. This growth is expected to be driven by the strengthening of
trends already underway in public and private investment (particularly
in defence and AI) and in exports (trade in services and intra-zone trade

in goods). Household consumption is likely to be resilient too. According
to our forecasts, job creation would continue to underpin household
incomes, thereby cushioning the impact of inflation.
A NEW LEASE OF LIFE FOR INDUSTRY
The Eurozone is undergoing one of the most profound industrial trans-
formations in its history. This is giving rise to divergences between key
sectors but, above all, to new drivers of growth whose effects are beco-
ming increasingly apparent (see our analysis). Indeed, the signs of an

economic recovery are growing stronger. Business sentiment indicators
show a marked improvement in the economic outlook across most sec-
tors (Chart 2), driven primarily by electronics (AI, digitalisation), the
automotive sector and aerospace. European industry is thus benefi -
ting from strong demand for capital goods; a structural trend that will
continue to be fuelled by ongoing investment cycles in AI, defence and
electrification. The introduction of minimum European content quotas

in the draft law on the industrial accelerator, together with the Com -
mission’s intention to tighten the criteria for access to European public
procurement markets, will provide additional support for European in-
dustry in the longer term.
FOREIGN TRADE: A REPOSITIONING THAT IS PAYING OFF
Following a decline in 2026, linked to rising energy import costs, the
trade surplus is expected to rise to around 3.0% of GDP in 2027 (of
which 2% of GDP for goods and 1% for services). Growth in European ex-

ports has slowed compared with its pre-Covid trend and is being ham-
pered by the decline in Chinese and US markets. However, Eurozone
exports in volume rose significantly in Q2 (+3.4% q/q).
More importantly, since Liberation Day (April 2025), Eurozone coun -
tries have found significant sources of growth amongst other European
partners, which has enabled them to cushion the decline in exports to
certain third markets. This is evident in exports to the rest of the EU,

EFTA countries (Switzerland in particular) and, to a lesser extent, the
United Kingdom, Ukraine and the Balkans (Chart 3). The momentum of
intra-European trade therefore remains very strong. It is expected to
strengthen further with the anticipated investments in defence, AI and
electrification.
FINANCING: FINANCIAL CONDITIONS ARE EXPECTED TO REMAIN TIGHT
Financial conditions in the Eurozone remained tight in Q2 2026, accor-
ding to indices calculated by the ECB (including the BIG [Broad Inter -

mediation Gauge]). They are expected to tighten more significantly in
the second half of the year: i/ the rise in long-term bond yields, which
has accelerated since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran, is expected to
continue; and ii/ the ECB will continue its monetary tightening (+25 bps

The bank

world
in Q3, then in Q4). For the time being, the debt burden is still weighing
only moderately on public finances due to the long average maturity of
countries’ debt (8.2 years on average in the Eurozone in July), but this
burden will increase by 2030.
On the corporate front, the rise in interest rates has remained contained
(+2 bps since January 2026, reaching 3.9% in July), which has contri -

buted to the strong growth in new investment loans (cumulated over
one year, +9.1% y/y in July compared with 15.3% y/y in September 2025).
By contrast, cumulative net debt securities issues over one year re -
mained stable in July, whilst cumulative net issues of listed shares over
one year halved month on month.
Rising interest rates will continue to hamper new variable-rate
home loans to households (around 65% of new lending) more signi -
ficantly than fixed-rate loans, for which rates stood in July at 3.69%

(+4 bps m/m) and 3.36% (+2 bps m/m) respectively. New home loans,
which are stable in 2026 on a cumulative over one year basis, are there-
fore unlikely to see any improvement. A fall in real-estate transactions
could weigh on new consumer credit through a reduction in expenditure
on home furnishings and renovations, whereas consumer credit has
been fairly buoyant so far (year-to-date, +5.2% y/y in July compared
with +1.4% y/y in January).
LABOUR MARKET: RESILIENCE YET TO BE CONFIRMED

Employment in the Eurozone rose again by an average of 0.1% q/q in
the first half of 2026, driven by Spain and Portugal. Given the growth
outlook in these two countries, this momentum is likely to continue,
whilst business sentiment surveys in Germany suggest that employ -
ment is beginning to stabilise. Nevertheless, employment levels are
plateauing (France, the Netherlands) or even falling in other countries
(Austria, Finland), which is likely to slow the decline in the Eurozone

unemployment rate, meaning it is expected to remain close to current
levels in 2027. Furthermore, second-round effects of the rise in inflation
on wages cannot be ruled out: the ECB’s wage tracker suggests wage
growth of close to 3% by summer 2027 (2.9% y/y in June 2027, compared
with 2.6% in September).
INFLATION: A PEAK EXPECTED IN LATE 2026
Changes in wage dynamics will be a key factor in the inflation out -
look for 2027, whilst energy price rises are picking up again, driven

by the conflict in the Middle East. This rise is even sharper than in
the spring for gas (linked to restocking ahead of winter) and is likely
to feed through to electricity prices in several countries. We therefore
anticipate inflation peaking at 3.7% y/y in Q4 2026, before easing as the
energy component falls. On an annual average, headline inflation is
expected to stand at 3% in 2026 and then at 2.8% in 2027.
At this stage, the pass-through of rising energy prices to other com -

ponents of the consumer price index, particularly services, remains li -
mited. The alternative core indicators monitored by the ECB, including
the PCCI (Persistent and Common Component of Inflation, 2.1% in July),
remain close to the 2% target. Core inflation, however, is expected to
peak two months later than headline inflation (3% in March 2027).
Inflationary risks are nevertheless tilted to the upside. These are lin -
ked to food prices, which are expected to be affected by the energy

shock and weather conditions (summer drought, El Niño); they could
also stem from a delayed impact of energy price rises on underlying
inflation. The rise in prices of energy-dependent inputs (notably che -
micals and plastics) and those linked to the AI sector is expected to be
sustained. Growth, which we expect to remain robust over the coming
quarters, will also fuel underlying inflationary pressures.
MONETARY POLICY: TIGHTENING WITHOUT SLOWING (GROWTH)

Following two 25-basis-point rises (in June and September), we ex -
pect a third in December, which would bring the deposit facility rate
to 2.75%. It would then be in slightly restrictive territory (we estimate
the upper bound of the neutral rate to be 2.5%). This rise would be
aimed primarily at anchoring inflation expectations and, consequently,
at curbing the pass-through effects of inflation. Monetary tightening
would therefore remain limited. Indeed, the overshoot of the inflation

target would be temporary, and the operational framework adopted by
the ECB in June 2025 allows it to tolerate this, provided it remains brief.
However, it cannot be ruled out that the ECB might take further action
if the decline in inflation proves slower than expected.
PUBLIC FINANCES ARE CONTINGENT ON RISING INTEREST RATES
The fiscal impulse in the Eurozone is expected to be clearly positive in
2026 due to the rise in defence and infrastructure spending in Germany.

This will contribute to a widening of the country’s primary deficit (from
+0.9 pp of GDP to 2.8%, according to our forecasts). The improvement in
public finances is expected to stall in both France and Italy, at least in
2026, due to the impact of inflation on the debt burden via index-linked
bonds. This trend is expected to reverse in 2027 with less expansionary
fiscal policies. Nevertheless, the increase in the debt service burden
would fuel a further rise in the public deficit, which would thus reach

around 3.3% and 3.5% of GDP in 2026 and 2027 respectively (compared
with 2.9% in 2025). Against this backdrop, the Eurozone’s public debt-
to-GDP ratio is projected to exceed 90% of GDP in 2026 and reach 93%
of GDP in 2027, according to our forecasts.
Article completed on 15 September 2026
Guillaume Derrien

Thomas Humblot

► 美國期中選舉結果揭曉 ，民主黨掌握參 、眾

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整 ，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢 ，主要係因美國期中選舉後 ，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴 ，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正 ，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告 ，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達 675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性 ，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢 ，從2020年之後 ，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢 ，反應其抗通膨保值特性 。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消 ，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋 ， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。
黃金走勢vs美元指數vs聯邦基金利率全球央行最新黃金儲備量
分裂政府中東僵局，資金輪動黃金避險

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，凱基證券整理；註：右上產業回報相關性為各產業過去3年之每週報酬相關性
► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業 (如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長 ，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗 。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋 、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃 (Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力 ，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥 、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際 ，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置 。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低 ，有助分散投
資組合對單一 AI題材依賴 ，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強 、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入 相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率 利多創新藥與生技公司

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言 ，檢視各類股在最近股災表現 ，美
國成長股 、資訊科技 、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股 ，表現明顯落後大盤 ，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大 、跌幅最重 ，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期 、負自由現
金流量 ，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮 AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素 ，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理 、防禦股 、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲 。與主要指數相比 ，面對近月
AI科技股修正 ，同期間費半大跌 18%、那斯
達克跌幅 6%、標普跌幅 1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言 ，從2015年至今 ，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環 ，核心消費受影響性低 ，有鑑於資
產配置角度 ，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束 ，美伊衝突可望降溫 ，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面 ，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動 。短期可布局防禦股 、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響 ，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置 ，利率走升有利銀行股 。此外，隨財政政策支持 ，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材 。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入 ，公債殖利率回落 ，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 。建議以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理，日圓弱勢盤整
商品
 美國期中選舉結束，預期中東地緣衝突降溫，油價從近期高點回落，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。
